   - `func_8001FB08` - returns constant 23 (0x17)

[0m[2mThe[0m[2m assembly[0m[2m code[0m[2m reveals[0m[2m a[0m[2m critical[0m[2m detail[0m[2m:[0m[2m the[0m[2m original[0m[2m binary[0m[2m stores[0m[2m a[0m[2m3[0m[2m at[0m[2m offset[0m[2m [0m[2m0[0m[2mx[0m[2m800[0m[2m1[0m[2mba[0m[2m0[0m[2mc[0m[2m,[0m[2m then[0m[2m jumps[0m[2m to[0m[2m ra[0m[2m at[0m[2m [0m[2m0[0m[2mx[0m[2m800[0m[2m1[0m[2mba[0m[2m10[0m[2m.[0m[2m In[0m[2m contrast[0m[2m,[0m[2m the[0m[2m built[0m[2m version[0m[2m jumps[0m[2m to[0m[2m ra[0m[2m at[0m[2m [0m[2m0[0m[2mx[0m[2m800[0m[2m1[0m[2mba[0m[2m0[0m[2mc[0m[2m and[0m[2m stores[0m[2m a[0m[2m3[0m[2m in[0m[2m the[0m[2m delay[0m[2m slot[0m[2m at[0m[2m [0m[2m0[0m[2mx[0m[2m800[0m[2m1[0m[2mba[0m[2m10[0m[2m.[0m[2m This[0m[2m instruction[0m[2m reorder[0m[2ming[0m[2m creates[0m[2m a[0m[2m [0m[2m4[0m[2m-byte[0m[2m shift[0m[2m in[0m[2m the[0m[2m code[0m[2m layout[0m[2m,[0m[2m potentially[0m[2m causing[0m[2m the[0m[2m subsequent[0m[2m jump[0m[2m table[0m[2m entries[0m[2m to[0m[2m be[0m[2m mis[0m[2maligned[0m[2m.

[0m[2mThe[0m[2m discrepancy[0m[2m suggests[0m[2m I[0m[2m need[0m[2m to[0m[2m verify[0m[2m the[0m[2m exact[0m[2m instruction[0m[2m sequences[0m[2m and[0m[2m their[0m[2m offsets[0m[2m to[0m[2m understand[0m[2m the[0m[2m precise[0m[2m nature[0m[2m of[0m[2m the[0m[2m code[0m[2m generation[0m[2m difference[0m[2m.[0mI see the issue now. The original binary for `func_8001B9F8` has:
```
8001ba0c: ac470018  sw a3,24(v0)
8001ba10: 03e00008  jr ra
```

But the built version has:
```
8001ba0c: 03e00008  jr ra
8001ba10: ac470018  sw a3,24(v0)  [delay slot]
```

The instruction order is swapped. The original has `sw` before `jr`, but GCC puts `jr` first with `sw` in the delay slot. I need to use noreorder to match:
